The coupled-whole-body face of this lens is the sibling [[Whole-Body|Whole-Body]] doc's central claim. D1 carries the same refusal upstream of the robot body entirely — a bridging mechanism *is* a claim about where kinematic structure survives the human-to-robot hop, and D1's bet is that structure-preservation (explicit projection) and structure-discarding (learned gap) trade off by regime rather than one uniformly winning, the same regime-contingent finding [[Locomotion|Locomotion]]'s A6 already reports for MPC↔RL coupling.

> [!tip] Forgetting Is the Tax on Every Loop That Updates Weights
> B3 promotes catastrophic forgetting from a B2 risk-footnote to a direction, and it underwrites every other update loop here. B2's continual recovery updates ([[2510.02298|ARMADA]] pooled-intervention pattern) erase base skills unless B3's subspace protection holds; C1's per-embodiment fine-tuning is forgetting across *bodies*, not tasks. [[2605.15735|UAM]]'s **<5%** embodiment tax and [[2510.20685|C-Nav]]'s **+9.7 pp** replay-free retention show the tax is controllable — but only if forgetting is a first-class objective. [[2608.05799|XEWorld]] names the WM-side instance of the same tax and quantifies it starkly: few-shot adapting an action-conditioned world model to one unseen robot inflates LPIPS error on a *previously seen* robot by **69%** — cross-embodiment forgetting, not just cross-task, on the world-model half of the WAM pair B3's protection scheme has so far only been argued for on the policy half.

> [!tip] The Field Keeps Measuring the Wrong Half of Its Own Interventions
> B2, D2, and E1 each catch the field reporting a number that does not isolate the mechanism it is credited to, and each fixes it with the same move — a matched control that varies one axis. D2 finds egocentric+WAM co-training ablations remove the objective *and* its tokens together ([[2603.16666|Fast-WAM]] **91.8%→83.8%**), so the gain cannot be attributed; E1 finds the generation literature reports trajectory count and acceptance rate, neither of which says which *factor* of the corpus grew — and MimicGen's two source-scaling nulls, read by the field as "source data is saturated," both varied placement while leaving strategy untouched; B2 finds "cross-episode" memory systems measured only within one continuous deployment, so the claim is never tested at the boundary that matters. In all three the reported metric is a *proxy chosen for convenience* that stopped tracking the quantity of interest, and in all three the fix is cheap and already exists — a data-volume control, a diversity metric, an enforced restart. E1's case is the starkest: the measuring instrument ([[2606.20871|Geometric Entropy]]) has been published, validated on hardware, and simply never pointed at generated data.

> [!tip] Cluster A's Synergy Is a Gate, Not a Given
> The Cluster Overview claims A1's latent-consistency reward, A2's causal-importance step weight, and A3's physics-predicate loss co-train cooperatively on one shared latent — but this is asserted, not tested. A3's physics predicates are evaluated in *world coordinates* while A1/A2's signals ride *latent tokens*, so A3's loss may not back-propagate into the same latent the other two shape, and (per A1's own risk box) a discrete action head, a continuous latent, and a physics loss can gradient-conflict. So Cluster A's coherence is a *gate*, not a given: run the three-loss interference probe (A1's H7) first. If the losses cooperate, A is one synergistic mechanism; if they conflict or one dominates, A1, A2, and A3 are three sibling papers on a common diagnosis (dense local signal on a latent substrate beats sparse outcome), each defended on its own falsifier rather than as a stacked system.

---

## Benchmark Gaps

| Gap | Direction | Existing closest |
|---|---|---|
| Joint-vs-alternating co-training ablation on a fixed latent backbone | A1 | [[2605.21800\|stable-worldmodel]] (OOD harness, but not the joint-vs-alternating grid) + [[2603.25406\|MMaDA-VLA]] (single-objective joint, but offline) + [[2603.22078\|WAM-vs-VLA-Robustness]] (WAM-vs-VLA OOD-SR + latency grid on LIBERO-Plus + RoboTwin 2.0-Plus, but compares architectures not the training-coupling schedule) + [[2510.18135\|World-in-World]] (closed-loop WM-utility SR, but visual-WM and not the joint-vs-alternating grid) + [[2605.12167\|MoLA]] (the cascaded imagine-then-act mark to beat: **92.7%** [[2510.13626\|LIBERO-Plus]] OOD two-stage — so A1's bet reframes from "clear 79.5% OOD" (which MoLA already exceeds while staying two-stage) to "beat MoLA's 92.7% cascaded mark with a single latent co-evolving gradient") |
| Causal faithfulness of latent reasoning under compositional novelty | A2 | [[2606.02277\|RoboSemanticBench]] (reasoning-into-action faithfulness gap, in-domain) + [[2509.19524\|StepEval]] (per-subgoal SR-vector protocol w/ VLM-as-judge + κ-validation — the named methodology behind H1's LIBERO-Subgoals, but a blueprint, not a packaged suite); no turnkey suite pairs a verifiable subgoal-step decomposition with a causal-importance credit measurement on a robot policy |
| Physics-consistency of policy *action* sequences against a verifiable simulator | A3 | [[2512.11891\|VLSA]] (LIBERO-derived obstacle-perturbation Safe-SR: 32 scenarios / 1600 episodes, CAR + SR jointly — the closest named action-Safe-SR suite, but bundled in the AEGIS method paper) + [[2606.23686\|LIBERO-Safety]] (a second action-side safety substrate — physical + *semantic* safety with collision-free OOD demos and a parametric env, but scores collision/safety SR, not a physics-law-predicate Σ P_i → SR correlation) + [[2605.08567\|ACWM-Phys]] (WM-rollout physics, not actions); the action-side foils now span three enforcement modes — training-time geometry ([[2604.17896\|Physical-Feasibility-VLA]] / [[2604.01570\|FAN-Prior]]), inference-time correction ([[2602.01960\|GVP-WM]] / [[2606.14084\|SDN]]), and a planning layer ([[2606.17982\|LAGO-Policy]]) — yet none runs VLSA's SafeLIBERO physics gauntlet, so no benchmark contrasts a differentiable physics-law predicate loss against test-time/planning feasibility correctors on a common obstacle Safe-SR axis, nor measures physics-law-predicate Σ P_i → SR correlation (H5's ρ) or DPO physics-preference held-out accuracy (H3) — true field gaps |
| Joint WM-action causal-consistency metric on manipulation | B1 | [[2605.29360\|MiraBench]] (paired action-counterfactual probes, scene-fixed: Physics Adherence + Action-Following Fidelity + Optimism-Bias Detection — the action-causal probe structure, but scores WM reliability not regressed to real-fleet SR) + [[2603.22212\|Omni-WorldBench]] (WM-only) + [[2605.06311\|VISER]] (sim-real r, no action causality) + [[2608.11174\|VIScore]] (the validity-apparatus precedent — run-level disjoint calibration + cross-method/cross-task transfer — but on sim-MPC planning success, never real-fleet SR) + [[2608.13552\|PlayWorld-Bench]] (long-horizon interactive closed-loop rubric scoring, but VQA-judged not action-counterfactual); no suite yet regresses an action-counterfactual divergence against real-fleet SR — the ρ>0.7 loop the bet needs |
| Cause-keyed cross-episode-memory + diagnosis-conditioned recovery in a *trained* VLA loop | B2 | [[2604.18791\|HELM]] (integrated loop, but episode-local + uniform rollback, never evaluated on RoboMemArena) + [[2608.16889\|BATON]] (now the real RoboMemArena SOTA — **57.7%** TSR / **78.8%** CSR, beats even the ground-truth-memory oracle — so cross-episode SR on this benchmark is *no longer unmeasured*, but BATON's memory is keyed to subtask-handoff readiness, not to a specific failure's *cause*) + [[2505.12224\|RoboFAC]] + [[2604.07034\|KITE]] (diagnosis-quality floor, **+36pp** detection / **+33pp** localization on RoboFAC itself — the closest standalone diagnosis eval, but failure-labeled, training-free, and episode-local, no memory or recovery-action generator); the memory-substrate row ([[2605.10993\|ECHO-VLA]]) measures recall but never tests forgetting or localizes *where* memory fails — [[2604.11306\|Hierarchical-Episodic-Memory]] supplies the missing learned relevance-based forgetting (−45% memory size, −35% query compute) and [[2605.29341\|WorldMemArena]]'s four-stage write/maintain/retrieve/use lifecycle pinpoints the failing stage (retrieval bottleneck); the narrower gap that survives: no benchmark isolates *cause-keyed* recurrence (same underlying failure cause across separate attempts) from BATON's subtask-boundary-keyed recurrence, and oscillation reduction (H3) still has no benchmark — confirmed field gap, now sharper than before |
| Replay-free continual policy fine-tuning with bounded embodiment tax | B3 | [[2510.20685\|C-Nav]] (continual object-nav, **+9.7 pp** replay-free — backs the NAV half) + [[2306.03310\|LIBERO]] (the lifelong manipulation suite with FWT/NBT/AUC across 130 sequenced tasks — backs the MANIPULATION half H1/H2/H5, but single-domain IL fine-tuning, not a flat-budget shared-subspace-vs-expansion protocol) + [[2105.10919\|Continual-World]] (canonical continual-RL Meta-World task sequence, backs H4's RFT recovery stream); the table now has flat-budget single-shared-subspace methods validated on action policies ([[2605.06175\|VLA-GSE]] 81.2% LIBERO-Plus, [[2512.08333\|RETAIN]] +40% real OOD, [[2512.23017\|Merge-before-Forget]] constant-memory, [[2511.18085\|Stellar-VLA]] NBT 21.7%) + the canonical null-space mechanism ([[2602.21919\|Learning-in-the-Null-Space]]), but none runs the principal-angle / Fisher-overlap analysis H1 needs over LIBERO's lifelong stream — the geometric-forgetting law is still unmeasured on policies, so the shared-subspace-vs-expansion head-to-head (H2) remains a port, not a result |
| Zero-shot extrapolation to novel link structures (morphology-invariant) | C1 | [[2505.14986\|AnyBody]] (**0%** extrapolation wall) + [[2602.10556\|LAP]] (**>50%** zero-shot, untested on [[2505.14986\|AnyBody]]) + [[2605.20811\|Demo-JEPA]] (**0.36** one-shot) + [[2606.24049\|SPACE-Cross-Robot]] (EE-state-delta + online adapter, **92%** zero-shot UR5→FR3 — the closest *interpolation* transfer, not extrapolation) + [[2512.13100\|OXE-AugE]] (physics-augmented cross-embodiment data lifts unseen-config SR — the data-side alternative the bake-off must control for) + [[2506.09366\|SkillBlender]] (cross-embodiment whole-body loco-manip across 3 Unitree humanoids with balance/feasibility metrics — closest coupled-body substrate for the H5 handoff, but narrow morphology and a control-blending suite, not invariant-transfer) + [[2608.05799\|XEWorld]] (the sibling gap on the *world-model* half of the pair: a held-out-embodiment diagnostic testbed showing action-conditioned WMs are "2D visual pattern matchers" — pixel-space action cues like optical flow cut unseen-robot LPIPS by up to 57%, but few-shot adapting to one new embodiment catastrophically forgets a previously-seen one, **+69%** LPIPS regression); no benchmark measures SR-retention of an AnyBody-validated invariant onto a coupled body across families, nor isolates representation-invariance from data-scaling on the same extrapolation split |
| No shared human-video-to-robot bridging-mechanism harness (matched egocentric-hours, shared platform, precision-vs-OOD regime grid) | D1 | [[2607.08436\|EgoWAM]] (controlled within-family WM-target sweep on a fixed HPT backbone — the right precedent, but one design axis, not six mechanism families) + [[2602.16710\|EgoScale]] (names "data axis dominates architectural axis" as the consensus D1 tests) + [[2503.00779\|Phantom]] / [[2503.07135\|VidBot]] / [[2503.23877\|ZeroMimic]] (three independent zero-robot-data points, never run against each other); no shared harness exists across the six mechanism families |
| Compute-matched attribution of egocentric + video-WAM co-training's gain to objective-coupling vs. data volume, and to action-conditioning vs. mere future-prediction within that coupling | D2 | [[2603.16666\|Fast-WAM]] (the closest existing ablation — removes the video objective and its tokens together, cannot separate the two causes) + [[2506.21539\|WorldVLA]] / [[2503.00200\|UVA]] (the same confounded ablation on two other architecture families, plus WorldVLA's own unquantified action-conditioning claim) + [[2606.20521\|HumanScale]] (the matched-5,000-hr controlled protocol D2's harness borrows, but answers a different question — source, not mechanism) + [[2605.00078\|Being-H0.7]] (the latent-vs-pixel-target existence proof H5 needs); no study runs the compute-matched 3-arm attribution D2 requires, nor quantifies whether action-conditioning specifically carries the gain |
| No benchmark scores a demonstration *generator*, and none scores the **allocation** question it implies — how a fixed operator budget should be split between collecting new strategies and generating new placements. Every system reports generation success rate on its own tasks plus downstream policy SR, with no shared suite, no diversity measurement of the output, and no protocol that varies the *seed's* composition at fixed count | E1 | [[2607.21588\|AXIS]] (the closest infrastructure — 207 tasks, versioned 25/50/100% snapshots against a fixed held-out protocol, but built to benchmark *policies* on data snapshots, not generators producing them) + [[2606.20871\|Geometric Entropy]] (the only trajectory-diversity metric validated in sim and on hardware, but its corpora come from a parameterized ManiSkill3 planner and MimicGen/DemoGen sit only in its related work) + [[2406.02523\|RoboCasa]] (carries both human and MimicGen-generated demos on shared tasks — the one existing substrate for a provenance-controlled comparison, though it reports policy SR rather than corpus properties); the comparability problem is concrete, not hypothetical — [[2606.25939\|DeformGen]] had to reimplement [[2603.25725\|SoftMimicGen]] to compare against it, so even the family's own head-to-heads are not head-to-heads |
| Ego/exogenous-decomposed causal-consistency score on a genuine second-actor task | A4 | [[2605.12090\|WAM-Survey]] (the single-actor joint the field trains and audits) + [[2603.15359\|NavThinker]] (predicts a real second actor jointly with the robot's own action, but folded into one undifferentiated $o'$, navigation-RL only) + [[2409.00215\|Intent-Aware-Co-Manipulation]] (a real human co-carrier's intent estimated online, but via a classical estimator outside any learned WAM) + [[2608.04653\|CoCo-WorldModel]] (the field's own anti-hallucination objective, ready to invert into A4's own metric) + [[2505.14986\|AnyBody]] / [[2306.03310\|LIBERO]] (single-actor benchmarks with no scripted second-actor split); no suite scores an ego/exogenous-decomposed WAM against B1's own causal-consistency bar on a task with a genuine second causal actor |
